soda-brain / system

Every skill, as it is tonight

A skill is the one screen a model reads before it does a kind of work. Eight of them are compiled from your own sentences (a ledger per surface); two ledgers are read directly without a compiled skill; the other 24 are written by hand (two of those exist only on the box). Each card carries the skill's current text, readable right here.

Read this first

  1. Read it. Open a skill's SKILL.md (installed copy, or the mirror in task-land: same file). Start with drafting or hub, the two with the most of your rules.
  2. Say what is wrong. One sentence where you see the thing it made (the "your input" line on each card), or on Telegram. It is filed into the right ledger the same turn; you never edit a skill or a ledger.
  3. See which rule produced a line. Every rule in a compiled skill ends with a tag like [ledger H12]: that is row H12 of its ledger, your sentence, dated and verbatim.

The skill texts are embedded below each card ("Read the skill here", and for each surface "The ledger (his rules)") and open everywhere, claude.ai included. The file links open on the laptop only; the Markdown line under each link is the same address for Obsidian or any viewer.

SODA-SKILLS-MAP.md (this page as Markdown links)[SODA-SKILLS-MAP.md (this page as Markdown links)](file:///C:/Users/Alessandro/soda-brain/system/SODA-SKILLS-MAP.md)
The rule loop

From your sentence to the line a model reads

You say what is wrong once. That sentence is filed the same turn into the ledger it is about, in your words, with the date. A small map says which of your sentences belong together; the compiler turns ledger and map into one screen, with an example in your voice for each rule, and installs it. The code that makes the thing (a draft, a card, a CRM step, a phone notification) reads that screen on every call. Afterwards an observer checks what was made against the same rules and writes one line per check; those counts go back into the next compile, so what still goes wrong stays in front and what never fires steps back.

  1. 1

    You say it

    One sentence, anywhere: Telegram, a card comment, a box on a page, ((rule: ...)) on the daily page, the CRM row.

  2. 2

    addrule.py files it

    The same turn, into the ledger the sentence is ABOUT (you never pick it).

    addrule.py[addrule.py](file:///C:/Users/Alessandro/task-land/_system/drafts/addrule.py)
  3. 3

    The ledger

    Your words, dated, verbatim, never edited: one row H<n> per sentence.

    <SURFACE>-CONTRACT.md
    _system/[_system/](file:///C:/Users/Alessandro/task-land/_system/)
  4. 4

    The skill map

    Which ledger rows merge into which rule, the group, one example in your voice.

    drafts/skill-map-<surface>.json
  5. 5

    compile_skill.py

    Turns ledger + map + hit counts into one screen; checks itself; writes nothing if a check fails.

    compile_skill.py[compile_skill.py](file:///C:/Users/Alessandro/task-land/_system/drafts/compile_skill.py)
  6. 6

    The compiled skill

    Up to 15 rules in front, each tagged [ledger H<n>]; the rest under "Assumed". Three copies: installed, mirrored in task-land, on the box.

    ~/.claude/skills/<name>/SKILL.md
  7. 7

    Who loads it

    The code that decides, on every call (named in the skill's first line), or a session about to do that kind of work.

  8. 8

    The observer

    Checks each thing made against the rules after the fact; one line per check in rule-hits.jsonl. Never edits, never blocks.

    rule-hits.jsonl[rule-hits.jsonl](file:///C:/Users/Alessandro/task-land/_system/rule-hits.jsonl)

Back to step 5. The hit counts decide what stays in front: a rule that keeps firing comes forward, a rule that has not fired in its last 50 checked cases sinks to "Assumed" (still checked, back in front the moment it fires). A "like" becomes the example of the rule it fits.

# Drafting (compiled from the ledger, 2026-10-04)

## How to use this

1. Read this before writing, not after. The critic reads the ledger at registration, which is too late to stop a bad draft.
2. Register through the lane, never send. Box: `~/triage/venv/bin/python ~/task-land/_system/drafts/register.py <draft_id> --sidecar <path> --origin box` (from `~/task-land`). Laptop: `python C:/Users/Alessandro/task-land/_system/drafts/register.py <draft_id> --sidecar <path> --origin quick-claude` (normal session `--origin laptop`); drafts via `C:/Users/Alessandro/triage/gmail.cmd draft ...`.
3. When he gives feedback, the same turn: `python <_system>/drafts/addrule.py --rule "the rule" --quote "his exact words" [--soft] [--item <draft_id>]`. A rule he gave and you did not file is a rule you will break again.
4. When he says "I like this": `addrule.py --like --quote "the sentence he liked" --item <draft_id> [--maps-to H<n>]`, so the next compile has an example of the target, not only the prohibition.
5. The ledger is `_system/EMAIL-REVIEW-CONTRACT.md`, his words, dated, append-only. This file is its compile (`python _system/drafts/compile_skill.py --contract email --install`): never edit it by hand, the next compile overwrites it.
6. In front: what the hit counts say is still needed. Assumed: still checked by the observer, back in front the moment it fires.

## The rules in front

### Ask

**R1.** Top down: greeting, one line of warmth (or, in a cold mail, a minimal intro: who I am plus one line of what we do), then the ask, then why, in the same paragraph. The subject is the ask too, and a cold subject opens with the sender framing ("AI founder curious to learn ...", "Student-founder curious to learn ..." or "AI student curious to learn ...", never just "Student"; framings he likes: "Startup Italiana/Tedesca", "Healthcare founder" Italiano in Germania), then per persona: to a CFO/CEO/direzione concrete about the outcome (efficiency of the equipment fleet, AI to optimise clinical engineering), to a CIO/IT close to their topic (IT processes, device data in hospital systems, automatic reports), to clinical engineering the ask to learn; every cold campaign ships 2 subject variants per persona, split 50/50, visible as a filter on the board. *e.g.* subject "La data della chiamata con ELF", then right after the thanks "Volevo chiedere quando sara' esattamente la prossima chiamata con ELF, cosi' che possiamo preparare una breve presentazione sul nostro approccio al database" *liked (H57):* "Startup Italiana/Tedesca (AI per ottimizzare ing. clinica) - richiesta call" *liked (H58):* "Healthcare founder Italiano in Germania - richiesta di call per imparare ing. clinica in Italia" [ledger H18,H47,H49,H59,H61 +like H57,H58; memory feedback_outreach_ask_first_peer_voice]

**R2.** One mail, one ask, as a full polite sentence: never a fragment squeezed after a colon, never where a character cap is saved. Do not deliver in the mail the content of the meeting you are asking for, and do not stack a second, less urgent request. Every cold email ends, before the close, with the calendar paragraph: "Sono molto flessibile sugli orari. Ecco anche il mio calendario, se preferisce trovare li' uno slot comodo per entrambi: https://calendar.app.google/tp6uY1YH7m3uQT9A9" (English: "I'm quite flexible on the time. Here's my calendar, too, if you think it'd be easier to find a common slot there: <link>"); "uno slot comodo per entrambi", never "un momento in comune". The availability offered sits in the window the reader can act on: on a Monday "questa settimana", from Thursday on "in questi giorni", otherwise "la settimana prossima". During the US trip the mail also says where he will be: a clinical engineer gets Orlando and MD Expo on 13 Oct, anyone in a city on the trip plan (Trippy) gets his dates there. *e.g.* "Would you have 15 minutes for a call in the next few days? I'm quite flexible on the time. Here's my calendar, too, if you think it'd be easier to find a common slot there: <link>" [ledger H27,H45,H48,H52,H99,H100]

**R3.** Student email: the problem in ONE short sentence (a student-founder researching clinical engineering who learned that teams in Europe struggle with equipment data), at most two short examples in parentheses, the CMMS named as "(e.g. Nuvolo)" or their own; never "for months I've been sitting with", no anecdote. The ask closes on "from an expert who runs it, like you". Soft: usually say he wants to eventually build a solution around it. [ledger H69,H70,H71,H72]

### Tone and voice

**R4.** No AI tells. His prompt is a message to you, never copy for the mail ("Vengo al concreto", "In breve", "Per essere concreto" are banned openers). No sentence whose only job is to announce the next one ("il pezzo che possiamo fare subito non dipende da nessun fornitore" is banned by name). No "You are listed as ..." role recall, the role goes inside the ask. No "X rather than Y" self-description. A fact from their website is written as something you know ("I understand it's CMIA guidance to ..."), never "the website says". Never announce the message's structure inside it ("a bit of context first, then the questions", "X first because of Y"): write the thing. *e.g.* "so I wanted to ask you two things, as the contact for CMIA San Francisco" [ledger H19,H22,H92; memory feedback_email_no_role_recall_no_rather_than, feedback_cite_knowledge_not_the_website]

**R5.** Write for someone reading it for the first time, in plain words: lead with the logical statement, not a compressed insider example the reader has to decode. What the reader already knows (Accreditation Canada, JCI, sistema qualità) is named in passing, ideally in a parenthesis, never explained. No nominalised abstractions, nobody talks like that. *e.g.* "come intendiamo rendere automatica la produzione dei famosi KPI di cui parlavamo a Siena (Accreditation Canada, JCI, sistema qualità)" [ledger H36,H25; memory feedback_outreach_ask_first_peer_voice]

**R6.** Put in what they told him, by name: the colleague with his role in their words, the week they actually named, the other people met (they are in the CRM and the event notes). After the thanks, ONE line recalling what you talked about, not the inventory. An observation about them never stands alone as a compliment: it is subordinated and carries the sentence to something of ours. A deliverable we owe them is named with its progress ("stiamo lavorando su X, se mi mandi Y te lo giro"), never written around. A follow-up from a meeting is built from the FULL transcript (include_transcript), interrogated question by question (who owes what, register, every name, address, date, deliverable), each answer a verbatim quote; a dictated address is reconstructed and SMTP-checked. *e.g.* "Le proporrei una chiamata martedì 6 ottobre nel pomeriggio, con lei e con Federico Franconieri, visto che e' lui a seguire la parte piu' tecnica di dati e CMMS." and "Avendo voi controllo diretto sul collaudo, avete a un certo punto una fonte di verità, come l'ospedale con cui parliamo a Rheine, in Germania." [ledger H23,H28,H29,H26,H87,H90]

**R7.** Match credentials and proof to this reader, per message: HEC is cut in front of a CIO and TU Munich kept; a CFO gets a number, a clinical engineer the operational reality, a CIO the integration story. No HEC line in an Italian cold email: HEC only inside an "about us" paragraph to a CFO or a director with an economics background, and when there is no such paragraph it is cut. Spare room under a length cap goes to specification (the wedge and why it matters there), never to adjectives. *e.g.* to a CFO, "help clinical engineers save 10-20% of their service spend, by (1) right-sizing the service contracts, (2) improving buy-vs-service decisions and (3) spotting recurring use error" [ledger H39,H40,H42,H53]

**R8.** His edit is the base: a missing full stop, a doubled space or a typo in his text is fixed silently when saving it; wording and claims stay exactly his. [ledger H44]

### Structure and punctuation

**R9.** Flowing narrative: no bullet lists in a mail to a person, zero "Termine: spiegazione" colons in the prose, no em dashes. The one colon allowed introduces the forwardable blurb, a separate italic block after "Due righe su di noi a seguire:". An em dash is a defect to fix before registering, in English too, never a style nuance. *e.g.* "Avevamo parlato dei problemi che avete a tenere i dati nel CMMS in ordine." and, as its own block, "Due righe su di noi a seguire:" with the blurb in italics [ledger H20,H21,H5,H30,H93]

**R10.** End with the closing formula on one line and "Alessandro" alone on the next; the drafter never writes a signature block: on the tundra account gmail.py appends the Tundra signature block under that line on every draft, send and update (never pass --no-signature; the critic judges only the text above it and blocks a tundra draft that has none), on the other accounts Gmail's per-account signature does it. Italian keeps the full close "Grazie e cordiali saluti," even when his draft ends with a bare "Grazie,"; English "Kind regards," or "Many thanks,". *e.g.* "Grazie e cordiali saluti," then "Alessandro" [ledger H17,H32,H94,H54,H88; memory feedback_email_no_signature_block, feedback_professor_email_style]

**R11.** A date he would say out loud is written the way he says it: under a week old "last Friday" / "lunedì scorso", around two weeks "a couple of weeks ago" / "un paio di settimane fa", past three weeks "a few weeks ago" plus the date. The absolute day is for anything older than that and for every FUTURE appointment, which must never be relative: "oggi", "l'altro ieri", "di lunedì" rot the moment the draft waits a day, so re-read any draft older than 24 hours before it moves. A PAST meeting or call is never dated at all (no month, no day): "a few weeks ago" and stop; this supersedes the "plus the date" above, absolute dates stay only for future appointments. *e.g.* "Grazie per l'incontro di venerdì scorso" for a recent one; "Le proporrei una chiamata martedì 6 ottobre nel pomeriggio" for a future slot [ledger H24,H97,H102]

### Sources and claims

**R12.** Only approved claims. Traction: "We are negotiating our first pilots in Germany and Italy." or "We're closing pilots with hospitals in Europe.", and in Italian, present tense, "Stiamo aiutando un gruppo ospedaliero in Germania a tagliare il 20% dei loro costi di ing. clinica". In a founder-voice email the company is named with what it does in one line: "costruiamo Tundra Health, una piattaforma di device intelligence che aiuta gli ing. clinici negli ospedali ad estrarre valore dai loro dati (CMMS, contratti di servizio) con l'IA", never the bare "una piattaforma di device intelligence". The figure: "10-20% del valore dei contratti di manutenzione" (the 30% is withdrawn). The product: one plain phrase, "uno strato di IA sopra il gestionale che l'ospedale ha gia', senza sostituirlo", never "predictive maintenance", never a feature list. A figure you introduce carries a source; his own numbers pass untouched. The reference customer is named: "l'ospedale con cui parliamo a Rheine, in Germania". *e.g.* "un layer di IA sopra il CMMS dell'ospedale che rende interrogabili i dati di manutenzione, i contratti e i manuali, senza sostituire il gestionale che avete gia'" [ledger H13,H33,H43,H14,H6,H31,H55,H60; memory feedback_cold_email_operator_ask_for_advice]

### Thread hygiene

**R13.** Find the thread first. A to-do naming a person means search every account for the existing conversation and reply in it (`--thread-id`), account matched to the context (Tundra business = tundra); a new mail only when no thread exists or he says so. Before threading, read who is on it; when someone joins the Cc assume the quote carries history they were never on: `--no-quote` plus one line of context, then grep the created draft. Before drafting read every whole conversation with them (WhatsApp, every Gmail account, Notion, CRM notes), never a name grep, and write on the channel or to the assistant they named. Check whether they actually wrote back: no reply on file = a follow-up to an unanswered mail, never a thanks for replying. Reply under the LAST mail of the thread, even when it is his own. *e.g.* `gmail.py search --account tundra "<name>"`, then `gmail.py draft --account tundra --thread-id <id> --no-quote --body-file <file>` [ledger H7,H8,H95,H101,H103; memory feedback_email_todo_find_existing_thread, feedback_thread_reply_leaks_internal_comms]

### Language

**R14.** Write in the recipient's language. "student founder", "fellow founder", "healthcare", "learnings" stay in English to founders. The university is "Technical University of Munich" when there is room, "TU Munich" when tight, and "TUM" only to someone working in Germany or in tech, never to an Italian or other non-German reader. *e.g.* "sono uno student founder a TUM, costruiamo un copilota AI per l'ingegneria clinica in ospedale" to a founder in Germany; "at the Technical University of Munich" to everyone else [ledger H15,H16,H50; memory feedback_outreach_ask_first_peer_voice, feedback_cold_email_operator_ask_for_advice]

**R15.** Italian: "Buongiorno <nome>," plus tu throughout after a real call, a warmth line, their own words mirrored back; "Gentile Dott./Ing. <Cognome>," plus Lei on a cold first reach; Umberto Nocco is tu. Cold outreach in Italian (email and LinkedIn note) never greets by first name: "Salve" or "Gentile" plus title and surname (Ing., Dott./Dott.ssa, Prof.; no title known = "Gentile <Nome> <Cognome>"), never "Buongiorno", every salutation checked by a native-speaker reviewer. After a call the register is read, never assumed: the meeting transcript and the thread decide ("mi dice lei", "Gentile Ing." = Lei), the quoted evidence goes in the sidecar, tu only when both sides switched. *e.g.* "Ti ringrazio per il tempo al convegno AIIC, l'ho trovato molto istruttivo." [ledger H34,SOFT,H64,H89; memory feedback_italian_practitioner_followup_style]

## Assumed (still checked by the observer, not in front)

- Campaign mail. Daily US board: subject exactly "Quick question on clinical engineering in your hospital (<hospital name>)", one sentence naming a system or vendor the reader runs (the CMMS from a job post, else an OEM such as GE HealthCare), named people, a department mailbox at most 4 of 30 researched and 2 of 10 sent. Italian subject "Due domande su processi IT e ing. clinica - <ospedale>" (plain name) as a 50% arm. The sender appends the founder or student signature (gtm-eng/signatures). Every A/B he names is tagged per mail where the send is logged. Every sent thread gets the label Campaign/<board slug> (all daily boards: Campaign/daily). LinkedIn invite with a note accepted and 24 h silent: next business day the DM is exactly "Just following up, in case you missed my last message. I'd love to have a call. Here is the link to my calendar in case it helps:" plus the link. A "not interested" or "remove me" is honoured at once, suppressed 12 months, noted on the row. A hard bounce is final: address dead on the board and the CRM the same day, never mailed again, the step becomes a SEARCH. [ledger H65,H67,H68,H73,H74,H75,H77,H96]
- Ask to learn, and say where and from whom. An observation is offered as something you are learning, never as a diagnosis of their problem; our objective is stated plainly (researching the work, with the objective to build a solution), the pain we intend to sell against is never named; no word repeated inside the ask sentence. [ledger H51,H37,H38]
- Reciprocity, not supplication: pair the ask to learn with what he gives back ("vorrei imparare da te come vendere meglio a ospedali, e condividere la mia esperienza con un fellow founder italiano!"). [ledger SOFT; memory feedback_outreach_ask_first_peer_voice]
- Someone who already accepted gets a reminder (date, time, link, what is set up, at most one ask they can ignore), never "does that still work for you?" and never an offered tech check. [memory feedback_remind_dont_reconfirm]
- Nothing that sounds like selling: never "I am not trying to sell you anything", never an apology for writing, never their problems presumed back at them, never the reader as the user or beneficiary of the product. The product is described in the third person, naming the role or the hospital; "you" stays for questions about their work today. [ledger H10,H11,H12,H46; memory feedback_cold_email_operator_ask_for_advice]
- Never the recipient in the third person or by surname inside a message to them. Research written ABOUT a person becomes "you", hedged ("I noticed", "ho visto che"), and the facts that existed only for our briefing (bed count, tenure, a scraped number) are deleted: a letter, not a dossier. [ledger H41]
- Self-framing by audience: student-founder conveyed, not stated, to a cold operator ("co-founder" reads salesy); co-founder to someone giving intros and to an investor, and an investor mail opens with the vision, "intelligence layer for medical equipment". [ledger SOFT; memory feedback_cold_email_operator_ask_for_advice]
- Mechanics: one long line per paragraph with a blank line between, never hard-wrapped at ~74 chars; `*bold*` and `<http://url>` are the plaintext conventions; `--body-file` or a heredoc for anything multi-line. Real accented characters, always: `e'` `a'` `o'` are NOT accents. Italian è à ò ù ì é in the right direction (perché, più, già, così, città, può), French é è ê à ù ç î ô (données, déjà, être, hôpital). An apostrophe standing in for an accent is a defect in the draft, the sidecar and the message. gmail.py also fixes Italian accents deterministically on every draft, send and update (_fix_italian_accents). [ledger H3,H4,H98,H91]
- The lane: a draft is a real Gmail draft, never chat text or clipboard; on the laptop the very next call opens the printed `Open:` URL (the box queues it for the laptop and pings Telegram); no "dsend" and no quoted text in the request means draft, then STOP; read this skill before writing, the critic at registration is too late. Every address on a GTM board or outreach list is SMTP-checked first (`gtm-eng/tools/smtpcheck.py`, RCPT probe with a random-address control): only "valid" or an address literally published online gets a draft, never a catch-all, unknown or invalid guess. Before creating a draft, list the Gmail drafts of every account plus the lane queue and sidecars by recipient, subject and thread; if one exists revise it, never a second. [ledger H1,H2,H9,H35,H63,H78]
- Professors: "Sehr geehrter Prof. <Surname>," when German-speaking; the intro is "a Master in Management and Innovation (MiMI) student at TUM, currently part of CDTM and the TUM Entrepreneurial Masterclass"; close "Kind regards,". [ledger SOFT; memory feedback_professor_email_style]
- Warm intro: "I'm writing to you on X's recommendation" (never "X suggested I reach out"), ask for their "expert opinion" (never "view" or "perspective"), drop the contrastive tail; when a mutual exists the referral goes before identity. [ledger SOFT; memory feedback_warm_intro_phrasing, feedback_outreach_ask_first_peer_voice]
- French hospital or medtech subject default: "Votre avis d'expert sur les dispositifs médicaux à l'hôpital". [ledger SOFT]

## Reference examples (his likes, not yet mapped to a rule)

- "Siamo due alumni della Technical University of Munich e costruiamo Tundra Health, una piattaforma di device intelligence che aiuta gli ing. clinici negli ospedali ad estrarre valore dai loro dati (CMMS, contratti di servizio) con l'IA. Stiamo aiutando un gruppo ospedaliero in Germania a tagliare il 20% dei loro costi di ing. clinica." (privati-nord) [ledger H56]
- "Salve Martina, siamo due alumni della Technical University of Munich e costruiamo una soluzione AI che aiuta gli ing. clinici a trarre valore dai loro dati. Avrebbe 15 minuti per una call? Vorrei capire come gestite i dati delle apparecchiature, dalla manutenzione ai contratti di assistenza." (privati-nord) [ledger H62]
- "Due domande su processi IT e ing. clinica (nome dell'ospedale)" (privati-nord) [ledger H66]
- "This is better because it's more of a story. It says that I was looking for an expert, and then I noticed you spoke about it publicly. And then you add thanks the end." [ledger H76]

## Procedure

1. Find the thread first (R13): search every account for the person, reply in the existing conversation, match the account to the context, read who is on the thread.
2. Pull what he told you and what they told him: the sidecar instruction, the CRM row, the event notes, the calendar. Who else was in that conversation.
3. Write, top down, in the reader's language, one ask, absolute dates, approved claims only, ending with `Alessandro` on its own line.
4. Self-check against every rule in front before registering. The ones most often broken on 2026-09-23: R1, R4, R9, R6, R11.
5. Register through the lane (`gmail.py draft` then `register.py`), then STOP. The hub card is his decision point.
The ledger (his rules) 95 rows of EMAIL-REVIEW-CONTRACT.md, verbatim

H1A "draft" is a REAL draft in the platform's drafts folder, never clipboard-only or chat text.`feedback_email_draft_must_be_real_not_clipboard`, `feedback_message_send_protocol` · HARD - mechanics (deterministic, no LLM judgement needed)
H2The moment `gmail.py draft` returns a `message_id`, the **very next tool call, same turn**, opens the printed `Open:` URL. Never hand-build that URL: it needs BOTH `/u/0/` and `?authuser=<email>`. "Opened" is not true until visually verified.`feedback_open_email_drafts_in_comet` (3 recorded lapses) · HARD - mechanics (deterministic, no LLM judgement needed)
H3Never hard-wrap paragraphs at ~74 chars. One long line per paragraph, blank line between. Gmail wraps narrower and hard newlines show as mid-sentence breaks.`feedback_gmail_html_rendering` · HARD - mechanics (deterministic, no LLM judgement needed)
H4`*bold*` and `<http://url>` are the plaintext conventions; they auto-convert. Use `--body-file` for anything multi-line.`feedback_gmail_html_rendering` · HARD - mechanics (deterministic, no LLM judgement needed)
H5No em dashes anywhere.global CLAUDE.md · HARD - mechanics (deterministic, no LLM judgement needed)
H6**A figure the DRAFTER introduces** (not supplied by him) must carry a source; a derived number inherits an invented input. His own numbers pass through untouched - the critic only FLAGS them when a source contradicts them (the 30% vs CDTM's own ~20% case), never blocks. Rescoped 2026-09-08: *"too strict for drafting"*; the rule exists for what I would make up.`feedback_deliverable_figures_need_sources` · HARD - mechanics (deterministic, no LLM judgement needed)
H7An email to-do naming a bare first name: search ALL accounts for an existing thread FIRST. If one exists it is almost always a REPLY (`--thread-id`), not a new mail. Match the account to the context.`feedback_email_todo_find_existing_thread` · HARD - mechanics (deterministic, no LLM judgement needed)

H10Never write "I am not trying to sell you anything" - *"who tf says that? its so suspicious."*`feedback_cold_email_operator_ask_for_advice` · HARD - content

H13No invented traction stats. **La cifra approvata e' 10-20% del valore contratti (H33); il 30% e' ritirato.** The ONLY approved traction lines: **"We are negotiating our first pilots in Germany and Italy."** and, since 2026-09-23, **"We're closing pilots with hospitals in Europe."** (H43)same · HARD - content
H14No feature lists, one plain phrase. **CORRECTED 2026-09-23: that phrase is NOT "predictive maintenance".** The old wording of this rule is where the critic kept pulling it from, and it re-entered the Di Marcoberardino draft twice after he had had it removed. We do not promise prediction: it contradicts Nocco's own "tomorrow" column, and Rheine parking intelligence is future work. Approved phrase: *uno strato di IA sopra il gestionale che l'ospedale ha gia', senza sostituirlo* (EN: a layer of AI on top of the system the hospital already has, without replacing it). *"prefer to keep it short also to not give out everything."*same, plus his ruling 2026-09-21 · HARD - content

Ruled 2026-09-08 (C3); replaces the professor-only version of this rule.`feedback_email_no_signature_block` · HARD - content
H18**Top down. The ask is the FIRST thing after the greeting and one warmth line**, and the subject line is the ask too. **Refined 2026-09-23 for cold emails (H47): a minimal intro (who + one line of what) comes before the ask; a naked ask straight after the greeting is abrupt. Subject framing: H49.** The reason, the credibility and the how come after it. Not "context, then context, then ask in paragraph four". *"io voglio farlo top down il messaggio... mi rincoglionisce che ci sia la richiesta [tardi]. Ho cominciato con quella richiesta e poi spiego perche' faccio quella richiesta."*his ruling 2026-09-23 (Nocco r3) · HARD - content
H19**Never transcribe his instruction into the email.** When he says "be more concrete", the mail does not say "vengo al concreto"; it simply becomes concrete. *"quando ti do un prompt e' piu' un messaggio che sto mandando a te, piu' che qualcosa che devi copiare paro paro nelle mail."* Banned openers of this family: "Vengo al concreto", "In breve", "Per essere concreto".his ruling 2026-09-23 · HARD - content
H20**No colon constructions.** No "Termine: spiegazione", no "Due richieste: X e Y". *"Tutte queste frasi con i due punti non mi piacciono."* Target zero colons in the body.his ruling 2026-09-23 · HARD - content
H21**No bullet lists in a relationship email.** Flowing narrative. *"cerchiamo di fare una narrativa fluida."* Lists belong in a document or a guide, not in a mail to a person he has met.his ruling 2026-09-23 · HARD - content
H22**Banned sentence, by name:** "il pezzo che possiamo fare subito non dipende da nessun fornitore", and every variant. *"e' una frase molto da intelligenza artificiale, che io voglio che tu rimuova dal tuo manuale di draft."* The family to watch: any sentence whose only job is to announce what kind of sentence is coming next.his ruling 2026-09-23 · HARD - content
H23**Name what he told you that they told you.** Not "il suo collega" but "Federico Franconieri, che segue la parte piu' tecnica di dati e CMMS". Se no diventa un messaggio molto blando e generico."*his ruling 2026-09-23 · HARD - content
H24**A relative date is perishable.** "oggi", "stamattina", "l'altro ieri", "di lunedi'" are true only on the day the draft was written. Write the absolute day, or re-read the draft before it is 24 hours old. Caught three times: Cevenini 17 Sept, Donarini and Di Marcoberardino 23 Sept.`feedback_standing_instruction_becomes_work_now` · HARD - content
H25**Name the schemes, do not lecture on them.** Something the recipient already knows (Accreditation Canada, JCI, sistema qualita') is named in passing, ideally inside a parenthesis. Explaining to an expert what his own accreditation scheme requires reads as padding.his ruling 2026-09-23 · HARD - content
H26**Un'osservazione su di loro non vive come frase a se' stante.** Mai "Quello che mi ha colpito di piu' e' che il collaudo lo fate voi." da sola: suona come complimento messo li' e si capisce che e' costruita. Va **subordinata** e deve portare avanti il discorso, agganciandola a qualcosa di nostro. Sue parole: *"deve essere subdolamente aggiunta, magari dicendo 'visto che voi fate il collaudo' oppure 'ho trovato interessante che voi faceste il collaudo, che vuol dire che avete una fonte di verita' alla base, analogo a quello degli ospedali con cui stiamo lavorando adesso'."* Quindi tre pezzi in una frase sola, la loro cosa + cosa implica + il nostro parallelo.his ruling 2026-09-23 (Donarini r4) · HARD - content

H35Read EMAIL-REVIEW-CONTRACT.md BEFORE writing, not after. The critic reads it at registration, which is too late to stop a bad draft being written. *"do you not have a simple drafting system or skill that you read each time you write something"*his ruling 2026-09-23, and the reason the drafting skill exists · HARD - content
H36Write for someone reading it for the FIRST time. Lead with the plain logical statement, not a compressed insider example the reader has to decode. If a sentence makes the reader reconstruct the situation before they reach the point, it is a riddle, not a line. Test: would someone who has never met us understand it without rereading. *"too specific this, it is clearly written without imagining that the person reading it is reading this for the first time. have to go for a clearer logical structure, not a specific example that people have to decipher"*his ruling 2026-09-23, on 'looking at how service contracts get renewed when nobody can say exactly what the fleet is' · HARD - content
H37Student cold outreach must answer WHY a student is learning this. Researching a role, a job, a workflow is a legitimate reason and reads as research; opening on a narrow pain hypothesis is not, it reads as a pitch wearing a question. Three boxes, all IMPLIED and never stated outright: why you are learning it, that you have nothing to sell yet, and a believable reason you care this much, namely that you want to build in this field. Never name the pain you intend to sell against. **CORRECTED the same day by his own rewrite:** stating the objective plainly ("with the objective to build a solution") is NOT salesy, it is transparent, and it beats hiding it. What must stay out is the diagnosis of THEIR problem, not our own intent. See H38. *"this is a bad student one, because it's already very salesy... say at least you're researching clinical engineer work, with the objective to build a solution in the future... all said subtly, never directly"*his ruling 2026-09-23, on the student cold-outreach template · HARD - content
H38Say the observation as something you are LEARNING, not as a diagnosis you have already made. "I'm learning that device inventories stay inaccurate" is a finding offered for correction; "learning WHY inventories stay inaccurate" presupposes it and reads as our thesis in disguise. The first invites the expert to correct you, which is the whole point of the call. *"I'm learning that device inventories in hospitals stay inaccurate, with information stuck in people's heads or unstructured in CMMS"*his own rewrite 2026-09-23, which is the version that works · HARD - content
H39Trim credentials to what THIS reader values, per message. He cut HEC Paris in front of a CIO because it buys nothing there, and kept TU Munich because it does. Never carry the full stack of affiliations by default. *"made it all fit by cutting hec (not needed in front of CIO anyway)"*his ruling 2026-09-23 · HARD - content
H40When a length limit leaves room, spend it on SPECIFICATION, never on adjectives. Name the wedge and why it matters there. A message that fits the limit with room to spare and stays generic has wasted the space. *"had the space, and was missing specification"*his ruling 2026-09-23, LinkedIn founder note · HARD - content
H41NEVER refer to the recipient in the third person or by surname inside a message addressed to them. This happens when research text written ABOUT a person (a CRM note, a board card, a prospect line) is pasted into a message TO that person. Converting to second person is not optional polish, it is the difference between a letter and a dossier. And while converting, DELETE the facts that existed only for our own briefing: their bed count, their tenure framing, anything they obviously know about their own organisation. *"Terrible mistake, terrible mistake I just spotted. You had named the contact by last name. When talking directly to him, referring to him as Alfaro. That was terrible"*his catch 2026-09-23, the Christian Alfaro / MedStar draft · HARD - content
H42Match the proof to the reader. A CFO gets a number; a clinical engineer gets the operational reality; a CIO gets the integration story. If there is room left in a message to a CFO and no figure in it, the room is being wasted. *"had the space, much better now with the concrete roi (it was for a cfo)"*his ruling 2026-09-23, LinkedIn CFO note · HARD - content

H44Obvious slips in his own edits (a missing full stop, a doubled space, a typo) are fixed silently when saving his text. H45The ask is a full, polite sentence, never a truncated fragment. No '5 min apres, pour apprendre de vous ?' or ': 5 minutes, to learn?' squeezed after a colon. A length cap (LinkedIn 300) is never where you save characters on the request: cut elsewhere first. Approved FR note ending: 'Je vous ecouterai a la journee du X le 28. J'aimerais vous parler 5 min apres, pour mieux comprendre comment vous travaillez ! H46Never make the reader the user or beneficiary of the product ('a record you can query', 'one record you can trust', 'that your team can query', 'the data your teams produce'). It reads as a pitch aimed at them. Describe the product in the third person, naming the role or the hospital ('clinical engineers and AI agents can query', 'hospitals can trust'). 'You' stays fine when asking about their work today. *"Hi Dan, ... an equipment record you and AI agents can query --> ... an equipment record clinical engineers and AI agents can query ... like you was too salesly... rule + adjust in all the relevant templ"*his ruling 2026-09-23 · HARD - content
H47Cold email structure: greeting, then a MINIMAL intro (who I am plus one line of what we do or research), then the ask, then why, in the same paragraph as the ask. H48Call ask wording: 'Would you have 15 minutes for a call in the next few days?', never 'in the next two weeks'. Closing line: 'I'm quite flexible on the time. # CRM (compiled from the ledger, 2026-10-04)

## How to use this

1. Two readers. A SESSION writing to the CRM (intake, a next step, a note): every write goes through `GET/PUT http://127.0.0.1:4124/api/data` or an intake-server route (`POST :4137/intake`, `POST :4137/task-handoff {action:"step"}`, `POST :4137/enrich-result`), never `crm.json`; a to-do naming a person is that person's step on the row, never a task. The READER (`crm-app/reader.js`, Opus, headless): input = this skill + the person's `relationship_state` + the events since `last_read_event_id` + the current step and its origin; output = one JSON object: `state` (summary, open_loops, cadence_days, context_facts), `step` {text, due, channel, who_owes}, `origin_why`, `match_of_previous` (same, partial, miss, n/a), `context_facts_new`.
2. The step text is English, starts with a verb, names the channel; a reply owed is said as `answer <first name> (<channel>)`; when they owe something, `wait for <what> from <first name>, nudge on <channel> if silent`. The origin renders on Today as `set by <you|the intake|the reader|...> on <day> from <WhatsApp|email|LinkedIn|the calendar|a Notion meeting|the to-do|by hand>: <why>`; a step whose `why` does not name a message, meeting or import is a failed row.
3. When he gives feedback ("this sucks because ...", "I like this", "this again", on Telegram, in a session, in the s/c box of the check board, in the Ask box on a row), the same turn: `python <_system>/drafts/addrule.py --contract crm --rule "the rule" --quote "his exact words" [--soft] [--like] [--item <person id>]`. A system change request ("change this on the go") is a build item in `~/.medtech-crm/WORKPLAN-20260924-crm-cleanup-and-monitor.md`, reported in the next system check, which for the CRM is at least weekly.
4. The ledger is `_system/CRM-CONTRACT.md`, his words, dated, append-only. This file is its compile (`python _system/drafts/compile_skill.py --contract crm --install`): never edit it by hand, the next compile overwrites it. The reader loads this file whole as its rules; the code enforces H1-H14 and the observer scores every Today render and every write against H1-H24.
5. In front: what the hit counts say is still needed. Assumed: still checked by the observer, back in front the moment it fires.

## The rules in front

### New, not yet mapped

**R1.** A draft produced from the person panel (ask box) is a real Gmail draft, never only text in the panel's Draft tab; if the person has a thread, the draft is a reply in that thread. *"this clealry doesnt work btw, i dont have that email drafted.... it should be a response, too."* [item: martina-andellini] [ledger H25]

**R2.** The ask box runs Opus with his full context (the same skills, memory and contracts a session has), and tells him in the browser tab when it is done. *"the ask function doesnt launch an opus claude.exe that has all the context of my stuff, otherwise it would know more. it seems to launch some shitty stuff. i want it to be opus, then i want a notificaiton from it when its done"* [ledger H26]

**R3.** A LinkedIn invite with a note that was accepted starts the clock: no reply within 24 hours = a follow-up DM (the fixed text in the email ledger) on the next business day, sent by the campaign engine for campaign people and by the CRM reader for everyone else; a reply cancels it. *"wait for a day and know they've accepted my request with a note, which means that now we have a conversation that has already one DM. But they have not done anything for 24 hours or more. Well, then on that next business day, you send a follow-up message"* [ledger H27]

**R4.** When someone at a hospital replies to a campaign, the others at that hospital are paused and he decides on a hub card whether to keep them paused and for how long: the card suggests one week; when it runs out the card asks again. Never a silent indefinite pause. *"it makes me choose via a card if i want to PAUSE that hospital and for how long (suggests a week, then asks again)"* [ledger H28]

**R5.** A reply that says not interested / remove me closes every sequence for that person, marks the row suppressed until 12 months later, and proposes nothing; the CRM reader never nudges a suppressed person. *"9 ... do the rest sure. dont ask me about it anymore."* [ledger H29]

**R6.** An automatic reply (subject 'Automatic reply', 'Out of office', 'Autoreply', 'Auto-Reply') is NOT a reply: it never pauses a hospital, never posts the pause card and never marks the person replied; a mailbox that says the person has left or names a successor is surfaced to him as a referral. *"I already received an email from a certain Holly and a certain Tim, both of which inside of them signal the best person to contact."* [ledger H30]

**R7.** A call he must make is not a to-do line: it is a person in the CRM with a phone number and a call step, so it appears on the Call page; the system finds the number. *"Even the calling ... You do your best to find the phone number. I believe the call section of the CRM is perfect for this. That's where I would normally manage all the calls I have to make."* [ledger H31]

**R8.** An inbound message from someone in the CRM is an EVENT and must land as one, on its own, without him telling me. The CRM must show it and the step must become 'answer what they sent', not the stale outbound step. *"he sent me a message so that's an event should prompt you to do something ... clearly there is a next step and then you could have interrogated your own memory and realized that I have to do something with this person"* [item: christopher-zubiate] [ledger H32]

**R9.** Every event is read, all of them (about 50 to 100 a day): each one pulls the person's history and context and is judged for whether something could be done; a hint is enough to move forward. Show more rather than less: a proposed action he finds irrelevant is corrected by him with one sentence, a missed one is worse. *"i wanted to really read each event that ever happens, because I think it's probably 50-100 a day ... every event kind of pulls the history and the context in an optimized way ... if it sees a hint of something that could happen, could be done, then it moves forward ... I'd rather see more than less, and then being able to say, 'Hey, this part here, it was not relevant' ... I'd rather have that and then correct each time until you finally learn."* [ledger H33]

**R10.** The event loop must be judgeable: every event leaves a line saying what the system did with it and why, so he can tell whether the system works, and he has a fast way to say 'you missed this event'. *"I want it to be iteratable so we can also judge it ... we can judge the system, see if it actually works ... I would also like a space where I can say, 'Hey, you missed this event.'"* [ledger H34]

**R11.** The Today review mode is a full-screen work surface like a GTM board: no side menu, no counters strip, no filter bar, one person per screen in large type. Nothing on the tab but the item being reviewed and its keys. *"the review page should be bigger. easier to read/work on like gtm board. and completely that only on my tab - no more distractions with a menu etc."* [ledger H35]

**R12.** A person with a step and no channel on file is a search task, not a dead end: before a card says 'no channel on file' the system searches every store it has (WhatsApp store and corpus, mail in every account, LinkedIn store, Notion, calendar, event notes, task files) for that person's number, address or profile, and writes what it finds as a proposed identifier with its source. 'No message to write' is allowed only after that search came back empty, and the card lists where it looked. *"did we not establish his contact? i remember clearly. if we do have it/a conversation on it somewhere, then its clear that we have a weak kortyx like system, because its missing a loop/goal objective of searching deeply for the things on which its missing somehting."* [ledger H36]

**R13.** A phone number he obtains in person or by voice is never just a message: file the person in the CRM with that number AND give them a next step whose text starts with Call, which is what puts them on the CRM Call page. Do it in the same turn he tells me, without being asked twice. *"It's very important that you also put them in the call section of the CRM. Whenever a case like this happens, they have to automatically be put there"* [ledger H37]

**R14.** Registrato non basta: quello che lui dice in chat deve finire dove LO VEDE. Un dettaglio su una persona (nome probabile, ora migliore per chiamare, chi chiedere al centralino) va nei campi visibili della riga, nome e ruolo, non solo dentro il blob delle note. Se sta solo nelle note, per lui non esiste. *"I said here in this telegram chat at one point a lot of details on this contact that I received from foch and you didn't record it ... I don't see the very likely name of the person which I had figured out in this telegram chat and I don't see it in the CRM"* [ledger H38]

**R15.** A reply is acted on at the next campaign tick, not when that person's next step comes due. When the reply comes from somebody else than the person we wrote to, on the thread of our mail, the person we wrote to handed it over: that person is stopped, the fact (who forwarded to whom, when) is written on both rows, and the hospital is paused as for any reply. *"you should be able to see from the email that it was fwd'd to him by someone else - a brunella of some kind. thats the person to stop with then no? and in general important info to have."* [item: emanuele-galbiati] [ledger H39]

## Assumed (still checked by the observer, not in front)

- The CRM owns every follow-up with a person. A to-do that names a contact is that person's next step in the CRM, never a task-land task; the old freeze (a `contact:` task owning the row) is retired. The one exception is an indirect, non-outreach to-do about someone, asked of a session as a plain to-do. "its really just always 1. lets then see the exceptions as we go." [ledger H1; memory project_coattio_v2]
- Every saved person has a next step with a date. A save with no step gets the default: `decide next step`, due in +2 working days, `next_step_default:true`, Ask on the Today card. No row without a date ever sits silent. [ledger H2]
- One writer. Every write goes through `GET/PUT :4124/api/data` or a route of intake-server that does that. No second process ever edits `crm.json` directly, on the laptop or on the box; the box only pulls. [ledger H3; memory reference_coattio_servers]
- Found contact details and batches are proposed, never written. A session that finds an address does not file it: the rake in the 23:00 sweep (`triage/contact_rake.py`) proposes rows in the sweep card and only an approved row goes through `POST :4137/enrich-result`; status honesty: sent to = `verified`, only in a draft = `inferred`, an office desk = `published`, `personal:false`. Contacts sync, event intake, Apollo imports: every batch is ONE hub card (yes = all, no + numbers = those), Notion rows only after that yes. "io mi faccio il bottleneck." [ledger H4,H5; memory feedback_found_contact_details_go_to_crm, reference_contacts_sync, project_event_contact_workflow]
- The output of person work goes INTO the row, and the row holds what is known about the person. The state, the found email, the next step, the note: in the CRM, not in a one-off page or a chat answer. What conversations taught (she is building a chatbot herself, he follows the CMMS data side) is attached to the person as `context_facts` and used when drafting to them. "L'output va dentro il sistema che possiede quel lavoro." [ledger H6,H23; memory feedback_output_goes_into_the_owning_system]
- Warmth is derived from channel facts (1 replied .. 5 nothing); override only with `warmth_override` and a note saying why. [ledger SOFT]
- An Italian name with U+FFFD bytes in the CRM is a data-quality bug, shown as "?" until fixed at the import; never guess the letters. [ledger SOFT]
- What he sees is small. The daily page carries ONE CRM line, `Today's items in the CRM (N)`, no per-person lines ("too much clutter"). Today shows only: due, coming up, replies to answer, campaign alerts; a stale step is shown apart, never as due; each row has the step and one "why" line (origin + open loop), reply-owed first. [ledger H7,H8]
- The commit is the yes, and a verdict is never blind. His one `crm-catchup commit ...` message (or a board commit) is executed as written, never a card asking him to confirm it. On the board, a and s open a one-line box: Enter = the verdict, text + Enter = the verdict with his reason, Esc = nothing; the reason rides in the commit (`skip | perche': ...`) and the executing session puts it on the row or in memory. [ledger H10,H11; memory feedback_commit_batch_is_the_yes]
- Every review of CRM items is a j/k board that follows the design system (`gtm-eng/DESIGN-SYSTEM.md`): one item = one screen at 1568x773, same zones, a/s/c decide, verdict advances, legend visible; the CRM check board is http://100.85.52.84:4143/crm-catchup.html. [ledger H9; memory feedback_review_page_design_system, reference_crm_catchup_board]
- Review by hospital, not only by person: the check board lists the hospitals I think he targets with the gap per hospital (not in CRM, no people, no attempt, no email) and asks which are missing; a missing name is a lead for a search in his mail and chats, whose result is proposed, never filed. [ledger H12; memory reference_crm_catchup_board]
- :4124 and :4137 are never started with run_in_background. Restart = `Stop-Process` + `coattio-serve.ps1`; on the box the cron pull restarts them. [ledger H13; memory reference_coattio_servers, feedback_local_server_start_pattern]
- Nothing consumes the Enrich queue by itself: the Enrich button only writes `enrich-queue/<stamp>-<field>.json`; a session runs `/overnight` or searches directly. Never tell him "enrichment is running" when nothing is. [ledger H14; memory feedback_found_contact_details_go_to_crm]
- One person = one thing to do. Never more than one open step or task for the same person; a new instruction about a person replaces the previous one (intake and handoff replace, never add; the bridge collapses duplicates). "only the last one is ever true." [ledger H15]
- Every step carries its origin: who set it, when, and from which message, meeting or import (`next_step_origin` {set_by, at, source, why}, source = `wa:<id>`, `email:<thread>`, `linkedin:<id>`, `calendar:<event>`, `notion:<page>`, `task:<slug>`, `manual`). A step he cannot place is a failed row, not his memory. "i dont remember this / where this from?" [ledger H16]
- Outdated check on every Today render and every read: a step tied to a date or event that has passed (a date in the text, an event the person's record dates, or more than 14 days overdue) is stale, flagged or dropped, never shown as due. A past date after a past-tense word ("no answer 21 Sep") is a reference, not a deadline. "the event passed bro! how can you not do that check." [ledger H17]
- Learn from what actually happened. Sent mail, WhatsApp, LinkedIn are the truth: a message he sent counts as approved and closes or advances the step (`step_done_outside`, channel-aware); his discipline in telling the CRM is not required. "if it actually happened, then we can assume it approved and good." [ledger H18]
- Infer the next step from his last message to the person: who owes what (he owes a reply, they owe an email, a call was agreed, a date was named) becomes the step, `who_owes`, and its origin. "My last message mentions a call that we've had, and the next step being him sending me an email, which he didn't." [ledger H19]
- Active cadence: while he is in a live exchange with someone (a message every day or two) the CRM stays silent; the reminder wakes 2 days after the LAST interaction (`cadence_days`, learned per person from the real rhythm, default 2), and every new message, in or out, resets that clock. A due date is never earlier than last interaction + cadence and never in the past. "if I write daily to this person ... I clearly am on top of it." [ledger H20]
- An unanswered inbound (an email, a WA, a LinkedIn message with no outbound after it) is the step, said as such, `answer <first name> (<channel>)`, before any other action on that person, and first on Today. "in particular gotta ANSWER her email. you knew that?" [ledger H21]
- Calendar events and the Notion meetings DB are interaction sources: a calendar event carrying the contact's email or name, or a Notion meeting row whose contact relation is this CRM person, is an interaction that resets the cadence clock and can close a step (a meeting closes a "call" step). A Notion meeting whose relation does not match the CRM person is not evidence for that person. "as long as its connected and true to the crm, too" [ledger H22; memory reference_coattio_notion_sync]
- Two kinds of input, both recorded the same turn: a rule ("this sucks because") goes to the ledger with `addrule.py --contract crm`; a system change request becomes a build item on the CRM workplan and is reported in the next system check, at least weekly for the CRM. He never edits a rule file. [ledger H24; memory feedback_rule_requests_are_binding]
- A CRM row is a proposal of state, not proof. Before saying "pending" or "never contacted", check Sent mail and the calendar; the picture starts on 2026-06-29 and covers only the accounts read. [ledger SOFT; memory feedback_state_check_sent_and_calendar]
- When a reply proposes an exact slot and that slot is free on his calendar, the move is to take it: a calendar invite to the person for that slot (with a Meet link) and a short reply in the same thread that confirms the time and mentions the invite, offering to switch tool if they prefer. Both are prepared the moment the reply is read and shown to him as ONE decision; after he approves, the system checks that the invite really went out before the reply is sent. *"he suggested an exact slot and it was free on my calendar. we have his email. so yea we take that chance. then we send him an email RIGHT or SOON (check that the invite actually went thru) short, mentioning the invite like i did."* [item: emanuele-galbiati] [ledger H40]
- A call that ended is an event the system catches by itself within 15 minutes (10 is the target): the call is written on the person's CRM row from the Notion meeting notes, and ONE approval-hub card proposes the next step; yes = the step goes on the row, a sentence of his = his sentence is the step, no = no step. Never silent on the row only, never waiting for him to tell a session. *"Dovreste essere in grado, in automatico, di registrare come nuovo evento la chiamata in se' e processare Notion con le note del meeting ... entro i 10 minuti da quando e' successo posso accettare un 15 minuti di intervallo, ma non di piu' ... dovresti essere tu proattivo e farmi una richiesta, di un evento, di un automatico next step, su cui posso darti un feedback in maniera semplice. Quindi penso che poi vada nell'approval hub"* [ledger H41]
- Chi CREA una card non deve chiedere cio che il monitor ha appena visto fare. Se sulla persona c'e un evento in uscita o uno step_done_outside DOPO il meeting, il tick non chiede il prossimo passo: la domanda e gia risposta dai fatti. Il controllo va fatto alla creazione, non ripulito dopo da hub_outdated: quando la pulizia arriva lui e gia stato pingato. *"its done already, you missed it being done a hour ago or so... system failure. how come"* [ledger H42]
- A delivery failure notice for an address (Mail Delivery Subsystem, temporary or permanent) stops every further campaign send to that address at once: the sequence pauses on that step, the row is marked bounced with the date, and one card asks for another address (H36 search first). Never a second mail to an address that bounced; the 25 Sep 'report only, never pause' ruling is withdrawn. *"YOU SENT AN EMAIL TWICE TO A WRONG EMAIL? ARE YOU FUCKING STUPID? CHANGE THIS IN THE CAMPAIGN DYNAMIC NOW."* [item: marco-pattano] [ledger H43]
- Once a person's contact route is established, write it with its evidence (verbatim quote + date + source) on their CRM row (and the to-do line), so the next agent does not search again. *"Yeah, I approve the process you propose. (process step 3: write the route and its evidence where it is kept)"* [ledger H44]
- A campaign send to a person must be stoppable from any machine, including the box, at once, and an organisation pause covers the whole hospital group (San Raffaele is Gruppo San Donato) including people outside the campaign he is talking to. *"why can we not stop antionia bianchi? lets make it possible."* [item: antonia-bianchi] [ledger H45]
- A call step on a switchboard or help-desk number names who to ask for: the person we contacted with their role (and when we wrote), plus any other named person found for that department, so he knows what to say when the line picks up. *"you should also include the person that we've contacted, just so I know and her role, so I know what to ask at the help desk. But also put the name of this person that you found."* [item: stacy-bolton-mylrh-org] [ledger H46]
- The CRM has ONE writer. Until the box becomes the single writer (S11), a conflict is never resolved by keeping one whole side: merge per record (newer wins per person and field), keep new rows from both sides, log the provenance. *"This problem is showing up again, so I'm assuming we did a bit of a mistake, again, having both being able to update it at all."* [ledger H47]
- A draft shown on a CRM review item is written for THAT step and in the recipient's language: Italian to an Italian (Caravaggi, Assing, +39), French only to a French reader; a draft 'written for another step' or in the wrong language is never shown as the step's draft, the row says 'no draft yet' instead. *"Okay, here's a big mistake, why is it in French? Are you insane? It's in French, and the card on the approval hub is so much better than this."* [ledger H48]
- The ORIGIN line of a review item is one plain sentence a reader can follow (who set it, when, from which message, meeting or import, in words); never a raw id (act:..., fact:..., linkedin:li:2-...) or a link he cannot read. The item carries a few lines with bold labels so the eye knows where to look, not a wall of text. *"what the fuck are we even saying in the origin? There is a weird link. I don't understand what you're saying in the origin. Can you fix it? Make it clear for me. If you want to give me any information, make it clear and visible. It's also so much text, I don't know where to look. Use a little bit of attention-capturing things that are text-based, maybe in formatting."* [item: amy-anne-fischer-01610816-adventhealth] [ledger H49]
- The review mode's left pane shows the full width of the state, the step and the message; nothing is clipped horizontally at 1568 px. *"I am not able to see fully horizontally, like the full width of the message on the left, which is very bad. I need to be able to see it all."* [item: amy-anne-fischer-01610816-adventhealth] [ledger H50]
- A mistake the reader finds (a 'reply' that was his own message) is corrected ON THE ITEM (the step, the flags, the draft), never only narrated inside the state text. The CRM drops cards like the hub: a worker (the twin of hub_outdated.py) rewrites or removes stale, outdated and mistaken items on its own, and an Update page at the top of the review mode lists every change and removal for 24 h so he can check them. *"You think the reply is your own and you cannot even change the card? This is bad. We need to have a card-changing system. ... You drop a card, and I have an update page (similar to the update page of my approval hub) where I can see the cards that were dropped on the CRM. ... a worker, that does the same as the approval hub worker. It essentially goes over the stale old ones, the outdated ones, the mistaken ones, and changes those cards ... put all the changes or removal that he has made into an update ... that I can click on at the top in the review mode."* [item: amy-anne-fischer-01610816-adventhealth] [ledger H51]
- A phone call with the person after the step was set (a call record, a meeting) closes the step: no reply card for a person he has since talked to. Family (his father) is never a CRM follow-up item: he catches up himself. *"I don't need to answer him, and you haven't quite matched the style. I've talked to him since on the phone, so there's no point; you should have seen that, it probably shows as a record that we had a phone call. He's my father, so that's something I'd rather catch up on myself."* [item: guido-sodano] [ledger H52]
- A friend (category CDTM, a 'how are you doing, man' conversation) is not a target: no review card, no follow-up step for them, now or in the future. *"No need to have Nick in the CRM in the future: he's a friend from CDTM, saved as CDTM, and you can see it from the 'how are you doing, man' in the conversation. He's not a target for the ecosystem or anything else, so I didn't need a card here. In the future, don't have it."* [item: nick] [ledger H53]
- In outreach messages, tell the contact where Alessandro will be: always offer Orlando at MD Expo on October 13 to clinical engineers, and when Alessandro's Trippy trip plan includes the contact's city, name that city and those dates. *"Yeah, my way is better because I'm updating them on my whereabouts. In the coming weeks, which you should memorize, by the way, I will be. You can always say that I'll be in Orlando in case it's a clinical engineer, because it will likely be there as well.  So you can mention Orlando and MD Expo on October 13. Then mention whatever their city is whenever I will be there. You can see the full trip plan on Trippy [he edited the text on the board and approved his version, see hub-edits.jsonl]"* [item: roberto-torres-jr-b3b549a0-stanford-children] [ledger H54]
- The review item shows the iteration log on its face: every earlier comment of his, dated, with the version it produced (not only inside the WHY pane); a rewrite never drops the proactive content of the version he liked, it applies his sentence to it. *"You had a much better text before than I preferred, which was much more proactive. In this one, instead of being proactive, you're sort of dodging and deciding to not send anything. Instead, you're asking them exactly what they're looking for. I had given you a very detailed comment, keeping roughly the same text that you had, but instead working on a pitch deck modification."* [item: vincent-carte-jacquesson-hopital-foch] [ledger H55]
- What qualifies as a CRM item: any message, draft, reply or follow-up with a named person (R1), whether or not the person is in the CRM yet (then the import is proposed first). A one-off hub item is everything that is not about a person: a system update, a to-do done, a batch, a failure, a question to him. *"If they're message related, and it's because people are not in the CRM, I would understand. Maybe the first step would be to import them in the CRM. If they're truly relevant, explain why they should be in the CRM so that in the future, that also becomes a rule, and something that the system does on its own. I want it to be another set of rules about what qualifies as a CRM versus a one-off approval hub item."* [ledger H56]
- The CRM has a 'Something missing / something wrong' button (every page, review mode included): it opens a chat line that becomes an entry in a system-feedback log shown on the CRM; the entry goes through the feedback session (system/work route: whitelist fix, build item, or rule) and stays listed with an empty check mark until it is resolved; his click on the check mark marks it done, it stays visible for 24 h more, and only his click on a small x removes it before that. *"There should be a button called 'something missing/something wrong,' and then it just creates a chat, and it becomes a bit of a log. This log stays there until with an empty check mark? Until that actually is resolved ... The check mark is clicked, but it will still remain for the next 24 hours, unless I physically click a little x button there."* [ledger H57]

## Procedure

1. Session, before a write: find the ONE row for the person; read its step and origin; replace the step, never add a second (the one-step rule (Assumed)); write the origin with the message, meeting or import it comes from (the origin rule (Assumed)); put what you learned about the person on the row, not in the chat (the row-holds-context rule (Assumed)); a found address or a batch is proposed on a card, never filed (the proposed-never-filed rule (Assumed)).
2. Reader, on every read: is the current step stale, a date or event already passed (the outdated rule (Assumed))? What did he actually do since the last read: a sent message closes or advances the step (the learn-from-what-happened rule (Assumed)); the last message says who owes what (the infer-from-last-message rule (Assumed)); an inbound without an outbound after it is the step, first (the reply-owed rule (Assumed)); a calendar event or a Notion meeting with this person is an interaction (the meetings-are-interactions rule (Assumed)).
3. Reader, the date: 2 days after the LAST interaction while the exchange is live, never earlier, never in the past (the cadence rule (Assumed)); keep his own step when the events do not change it (`match_of_previous: same`).
4. Never invent: only what is printed in the prompt or on the row. A read that fails leaves the previous step in place and is retried; a failure is a hit line, not a silent gap.
